{"batchcomplete":true,"query":{"pages":[{"pageid":2533,"ns":0,"title":"Doraemon (2005 Anime)/Episodes","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{2005 Anime}}This is a list of episodes of '''[[Doraemon (2005 Anime)]]'''. The series airs 52 episodes and one movie annually. As of 2025, a total of 896 episodes, or 1,494 segments including specials, have been aired. An episode usually consists of two separate segments, each of which contains at least one adaptation of the original manga. However, special broadcasts, such as Doraemon's birthday specials or the one-hour Doraemon New Year's Eve special, may contain three or more segments. New episodes are broadcast every Saturday at 5:00 p.m. (Japan time). \n\nAll content on this page is owned by [[Fujiko Pro]], [[Shogakukan]], [[TV Asahi]], [[Shin-Ei Animation]], and [[ADK]].\n\n==2005==\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size:small;\"\n!Air date!!EP#!!English title!!Japanese title!!Manga source\n|-\n| rowspan=\"3\" |4-15|| rowspan=\"3\" |001||[[The Fishing Pond in My Study Room]]||勉強べやの釣り堀||12巻\n|-\n|[[The Time Machine Disappeared!!]]||タイムマシンがなくなった!!||22巻\n|-\n|[[Remember! Those segments were aired in 2005 and 2022.\n==2005==\n''Doraemon Mini Theater'' (ドラえもんミニシアター) consisted of 11 segments. Those segments were aired in between segments of the episodes, starting from April 22 - July 29, 2005.\n{| class=\"wikitable\"\n|-\n|'''Air date'''\n|'''EP#'''||'''English title'''||'''Japanese title'''||'''Manga source'''\n|-\n|4-22\n|2||[[Doraemon Mini Theater: The Farewell Handkerchief]]||ドラえもんミニシアター：さよならハンカチ||大11巻\n|-\n|4-29\n|3||[[Doraemon Mini Theater: The Never-Falling Rope]]||ドラえもんミニシアター：おちないつな||大5巻\n|-\n|5-13\n|5||[[Doraemon Mini Theater: The Anything Rope]]||ドラえもんミニシアター：なんでもロープ|| rowspan=\"2\" |大18巻\n|-\n|5-20\n|6||[[Doraemon Mini Theater: Let’s Play by Turning Flat]]||ドラえもんミニシアター：ペタンコになって遊ぼう\n|-\n|5-27\n|7||[[Doraemon Mini Theater: Riding on a Ball]]||ドラえもんミニシアター：ボールに乗って||14巻\n|-\n|6-3\n|8||[[Doraemon Mini Theater: The Fireworks Seeds]]||ドラえもんミニシアター：花火のタネ|| rowspan=\"3\" |大18巻\n|-\n|6-10\n|9||[[Doraemon Mini Theater: The Magic Zipper]]||ドラえもんミニシアター：マジックチャック\n|-\n|6-17\n|10||[[Doraemon Mini Theater: The Robot Pencil]]||ドラえもんミニシアター：ロボットえんぴつ\n|-\n|6-24\n|11||[[Doraemon Mini Theater: The Shoe Set]]||ドラえもんミニシアター：シューズセット||大11巻\n|-\n|7-8\n|13||[[Doraemon Mini Theater: The Absorbing String Phone]]||ドラえもんミニシアター：吸い込むことのできる糸でんわ||大18巻\n|-\n|7-29\n|15||[[Doraemon Mini Theater: The Magic Faucet]]||ドラえもんミニシアター：まほうのじゃぐち||大4巻\n|}\n==2022==\n''DoraDora Mini Theater'' (ドラドラMiniシアター) consisted of 9 segments. Air Pistol Medicine|Japanese = (空気ピストル) - (空気ピストルのくすり)|Owner = Doraemon|User = [[Doraemon]], [[Nobita Nobi|Nobita]], [[Suneo Honekawa|Suneo]], [[Takeshi Gouda|Gian]], [[Yasuo]], [[Haruo]], [[Suzuki]], [[Yoshida]], [[Yamada]] and other friends of Nobita|Gadget Type = Pistol|Image1 = Air pistol.jpg|Image6 = Air Pistol manga.PNG}}The '''Air Pistol''' is a misnomer name for both the '''Air Pistol''' and the '''Medicine Air Pistol'''. Both of these are artillery gadgets. Their function is rather similar to the [[Air Cannon]], but less effective.\n\n== Function==\nThe Air Pistol is equipped on a person's finger. When they point out their finger towards an object or person, they can shoot by shouting \"Bang!\". The Air Pistol has lesser recoil, but also has shorter range and power when compared to the larger Air Cannon. The Air Pistol has a range of around 10 meters (about 35 feet).\n\nWhile the Medicine Air Pistol is rather similar to it, the Medicine Air Pistol looks like a big bullet. It stores a type of liquid that can be used as artillery when applied on one's finger. When a non-living thing is hit, they'll simply be pushed or blown away, but some objects may break, like a branch, leaf, or fruit. However, when living things like humans, animals and robots (that may sleep) are hit by the bullets, they fall asleep (anesthesia) or fall down. The Air Bullets function like paralysis darts.\n\n==Appearances in Games==\n===''[http://doraemon.wikia.com/wiki/Nobita_and_the_Green_Giant_Legend_DS?action=edit&redlink=1 Nobita and the Green Giant Legend DS]''===\n[[File:Kukipistorunokusuri.jpg|thumb|Air Pistol Medicine in DS game.]]\nThe Air Pistol (Shaped like a bullet) is used in the Challenge game \"けんじゅうおう コンテスト\"(Kenju Contest). The player controls Nobita, and may have up to 10 bullets at a time. To shoot, a player must shout into the microphone (the game prompts a shout of \"Bang!\", but any word is fine). The bullets can be blocked if they hit each other. When a character of the player is hit, they'll fall asleep, unable to wake up until the next game. NPC characters have unlimited bullets.\n\nWhen replayed in the Main Menu, the Kenju Contest is only played versus other players in Wireless Communication.\n\nThe Air Pistol is listed under number 18 out of 61 gadgets in the game.\n\n==Trivia==\n\n*The Air Pistol is generally equipped on a person's finger, but characters without actual fingers, such as [[Doraemon]] and [[Dorami]], have also been seen using them.\n*In the 1979 anime, Air Pistol is a cream that is used on a person's hands and do the same with new de-sign when first seen.\n*It has the same basic function as the Air Cannon, both of them shoot destructive blasts of air when a person shouts 'Bang!'.\n*An [[Secret Gadget Air Pistol|Air Pistol that functions as a TV remote]] has been made by Epoch.\n\n==Gallery==\n<gallery position=\"center\">\nAir pistol.jpg\nAir Pistol.PNG\n</gallery>\n\n[[es:Pistola de aire]]\n[[ja:空気ピストル]]\n[[vi:Súng không khí]] \n[[Category:Gadgets]]\n[[Category:Gadgets originated from original manga]]\n[[Category:Weapon Gadgets]]"}}}]},{"pageid":13643,"ns":0,"title":"Secret Gadget Air Pistol","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"[[File:Doraemon Kuuki Pistol Remote Control.jpg|thumb|220x220px]]\nThe '''Doraemon Secret Gadget Air Pistol''' is a remote control for AV equipment, made to resemble the [[Air Pistol (gadget)|Air Pistol seen in the Doraemon anime]]. It can be worn over the finger, much like how the Doraemon anime characters wear their Air Pistols.\n\n== Functions ==\nThe Secret Gadget Air Pistol has 4 buttons that can be programmed to control certain functions performed by AV equipment, such as TVs, VCRs and DVD players.\n\nTo program these buttons, one has to press the button on the pistol that they want to program, and then press the button corresponding to an action on the regular TV remote. The button, on the pistol, will then perform the action performed by the standard remote button pressed. For example, if one pressed button A to program it, and pressed the \"5\" button on the standard TV remote, the pistol's \"A\" button would then tune the television set into channel 5 (in most parts of Japan, this channel number is used by TV Asahi, Doraemon's Japanese broadcaster).\n\nTo use a button once programmed, the user presses the button (\"arming\" the pistol), and then says \"Bang!\", as if firing the Air Pistol in the Doraemon anime series. The action is then performed using the same infrared technology as standard TV remotes.\n[[Category:Gadgets]]\n[[Category:Gadgets originated from original manga]]"}}}]},{"pageid":13929,"ns":0,"title":"Nina","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{Stub}}\n{{CTab}}\n{{Characters infobox\n|Name           = Nina\n|Japanese name  = ニーナ ''Nīna''\n|Image          = Artwork Nina.png\n|Gender         = Female\n|Species        = Human\n|Hair color     = Moderate amaranth with Light amberish gray streaks\n|Skin color     = Peach\n|Nationality    = Foreigner\n|Habitat        = Space\n|Friends        = [[Nobita]], [[Doraemon]]\n|Enemies        = [[Mong]]\n|Personality    = Fangirl\n|Alignment      = Lawful Good\n|Idol           = [[Perman]]\n|Career         = Protecting the universe from Mong.\n|First appeared = [[The Giant Dora-Raccoon at Midnight]]\n|Japanese seiyū = Maaya Sakamoto|Color #1 = #DF013A\n|Color #2       = #FE2E64\n|Color #3       = #DF013A\n|Age            = 12+\n|Birthdate      = ???\n|Interests      = Perman\n}}\n:''This article is for the character in [[The Giant Dora-Raccoon at Midnight]].''\n:''Don't confuse this character with [[Nina (The Doraemons Special)|Nina]], Doranichov's girlfriend.''\n----\n\n{{Template:Quote|Yes, sir! Insect Pills\n|Next episode = Stuff Only for You Gas\n|Color = CC845A\n|Color light = FFA500\n|Border color = B37450\n}}\n{{EpisodeInfobox\n|episode= Mama’s Appreciation\n|color=  #CC845A\n\n|text color=|animeimage=|tcimage=|japanese= ママのありがたみ\n|romaji= Mama no arigatami\n|broadcast= [[TV Asahi]]\n|series=|airdate= April 27, 1990\n|season= 12\n|number=  1077\n\n|overall=|manga= [[Chapter 353:Machine that making appreciation|The Understanding and Thankfulness Machine]]\n|opening=|ending=|insert=|writer=|story=|animedirector=|director=|previous=|next=}}\n'''Mama’s Appreciation''' (ママのありがたみ/Mama no arigatami) is an 1077th episode of [[Doraemon (2005 anime)|Doraemon (2005 series)]]. \n\n==Plot==\nNobita leaves a lot of food uneaten, so his mother goes to his room to lecture him. This time, she thinks she won't scold him, but will try to persuade him gently not to waste food. However, the whole conversation is completely nonsensical. His mother says that when she is little, because of Japan's defeat in the war, she often doesn't have enough to eat. She asks Nobita what he will do if there is no food. Nobita says he will ask his mother to cook. His mother asks what he will do if there is no rice. Nobita says he will eat bread. His mother says what if there is nothing at all? Nobita says he will eat out, and then mentions a really good ramen shop and asks his mother to take him there. Seeing that Nobita is completely oblivious, his mother leaves a little angrily, while Nobita can't understand why his mother is angry. Seeing this, Doraemon realizes that Nobita, having never experienced hunger, can't understand. Therefore, he takes out the \"Understanding and Thankfulness Machine\" and first lets Nobita experience the importance of air. Nobita immediately feels excruciating pain after pressing the button. Doraemon lectures him, then realizes he's gone too far and quickly presses the white button. Next, Nobita tries the food. Nobita says he isn't a glutton, so it is okay. After pressing the button, he hears the sound of eating a snack. Nobita sees the cake and says the machine is inaccurate. On his way back to his room, he falls and breaks the cake, and is scolded by his mother for wasting food. Nobita wants to eat instant noodles (ramen), but finds they are out of them in the kitchen. He wants to buy bread, but realizes he has no money. He thinks it is okay, dinner is coming up soon, but then he receives a call from Gian asking him to play baseball. Apparently, one of them has to go home, so they ask Nobita to join them. During the game, the ball goes too far and Nobita can't find it. Gian and Suneo say it is a newly bought ball with everyone's money, and they have to find it. So Nobita keeps searching until very late. When he gets home, his mother scolds him, telling him the food is already put away and he should go to sleep. Nobita is very hungry and in great pain. Doraemon asked him if he understands the importance of food. Nobita pleads and says he understands, so Doraemon presses the white button. His mother then tells him the food is heated up and calls him to eat. Nobita eats very happily, saying it is the first time he's ever known food to taste so good. After finishing his meal, Nobita goes to sleep. While using the toilet, his mother reminds him that the kitchen light is on. After finishing, she reminds him again that the toilet door is open. When he is about to sleep, she reminds him that he has to finish his homework before he can sleep. Nobita thinks his mother is nagging, so he asks Doraemon to bring out the \"Understanding and Thankfulness Machine,\" saying he wants to know how precious his mother is. He uses the machine, and then his mother says she has an urgent matter and has to leave home. Doraemon thinks Nobita always uses the gadget in strange way.\n\n==Characters==\n*[[Doraemon]]\n*[[Nobita Nobi]]\n*[[Tamako Nobi]]\n*[[Nobisuke Nobi]]\n*[[Suneo Honekawa]]\n*[[Takeshi Gouda]]\n*[[Shizuka Minamoto]]\n*[[Gian's mother]]\n\n==Gadgets Used==\n*'''[[Understanding and Thankfulness Machine]]'''\n\n{{EpisodePrevNext\n|Anime series = 1979\n|Previous episode = The Invincible! When Nobita opened the box, he was delighted, and goes back inside the house and into his room. Doraemon asked Nobita to go back and help his mom cleaning the shed, and Doraemon asks Nobita what that stuff he took is? He showed the two markered drawings he drew when he was four years old. Next, there is a three clay design: which assembles him and his parents. Then, Nobita came up with an idea, then Doraemon foresees that Nobita's neighbourhood will change in 25 years later.\n[[File:Discuss_Everything_About_Doraemon_Wiki_Fandom_-_Mozilla_Firefox_6_3_2020_9_14_17_PM.png|thumb|298x298px|Nobisuke found an old map of the location of where the \"Buried Object\" is at.]]\nIn the future, we can see Nobisuke chasing after his friends, [[Yasashiku Gouda]], and [[Suneki Honekawa]], but his mother, [[Shizuka Minamoto]] caught Nobisuke. Then, after Yasashi and Suneki escaped in time, Shizuka told her son that he and his father would have a little talk later. Meanwhile, on future Nobita, he found his childhood manga name, \"Oshishi Mask\" while walking, and his phone starts ringing, which is his wife calling, and she said, \"どうしたの?\" (What's going on?), and he replied, \"Nothing, I just got off from work...\", then his face changed, when he realizes something happened while he was gone. Later that night, while Future Nobita and Nobisuke are having a conversation, Nobisuke asked his father if he received a lot of compliments from his teacher when he was little. Stunned by his son's question, Future Nobita apprehensively said he got a 100 test, every single day, but Nobisuke refused to believe what he said. Future Nobita gets up from his seat and tries to show him that he is telling the truth. He pulls the desk drawer and found one small box labelled, \"Important Things\" in Japanese, and he opens the box, found his old 100 test, and showed to Nobisuke. As he looks closer to his father's test, his eyes wondering if he's telling the truth, then Future Nobita tells his son to follow dad's advice and never give up on learning, and walks away. As he exits the room, he sighed, and that's his ONLY 100 test he ever took. At the flashback from an episode, [[W-What!? Nobita Got a 100!!/2005 Anime/Original|What!! Nobita Got 100 Points]]; little Nobita talks to himself to see if he's dreaming or not, he's not dreaming. At home, his mother founds out about his 100 test, and she tells Nobita to hang it on the wall of his room, ending the flashback. Nobisuke is still not sure about the test his father showed to him. He looks back at the box to find if there is any other 100 test Nobita took. Then, he saw something inside the box, which is an old map that shows the location of a buried, unknown object.\n\nThe next day, Nobisuke used the laptop to contact the Help Center to find out more information about the old map he found last night. However, Future Shizuka thought that he is studying this time and walks out of the scene. Outside flying, Nobisuke knows the location of where the buried unknown object was found, which is located at the \"Pine Tree of Yuradama,\" then he saw Yasashiku, and Suneki on the ground, and lands there. He asks the two of them if he can help him dig the unknown object buried in the Pine Tree Of Yuradama, then they agreed. The next scene shows a steep hill, which is where they at right now. Nobisuke grabs the map, and he says, \"the buried object is exactly right here.\" Then, Nobisuke asks the old man where the \"Pine Tree of Yuradama\" is at. The old man replied it was right \"there\" (where they're standing at). Nobisuke replied, \"Thank you,\" and goes to the exact location of the buried object that was hidden. Nobisuke tells Suneki to use the \"Mole Drill\" to dig out the dirt, and he said, \"Of course, I'll take it until-,\" but he interrupts him by hitting Suneki in the head, and gives his Mole Drill to Nobisuke, then he throws it into the sand, and starts digging slowly. While it's digging, he asks Suneki if he believes his gadget would work, and Suneki replied, \"How should I know?\", after a few seconds, the Mole Drill explodes. Instead, they gotta dig it themselves without using any gadget. At the house, Future Shizuka thank Future Nobita for giving Nobisuke a piece of good advice last night, and Nobita laughs softly while reading \"Oshishi Mask\" (his childhood manga). As they were digging, Nobisuke found the buried object (which is a small sphere). After they took the sphere out of the dirt, Nobisuke puts his Take-copter on his head and thank his friends for digging with him, and flies away. Suneki and Yasashiku were shocked that he brought the sphere with him just for himself, which Suneki realize it's just a trick. Random Person came by and shouted, \"What are you guys doing?\" which makes Yasashiku and Suneki crying. Back at the house, Future Nobita still reading his manga and feels delighted while reading, then he heard his wife calling his son, telling him where did he go this whole time? He replied while he's covered in dirt, \"Went out for a bit,\" and future Shizuka tells him to hurry up and take a shower, while the sphere is hiding outside the room.\n\nNobisuke goes inside the room to figure out the passcode of the sphere he found in the park. He tries typing \"807\" (Nobita's birthday), but the code is incorrect. Then, future Nobita came to check Nobisuke and asks Nobisuke what that thing he's hiding on is. Nobisuke came up with an idea to distract his father, by telling him that he caught his father reading a manga. Astounded by his son, he nervously drops his manga on Nobisuke's bed, and said, \"You know, adults don't read manga.\" and walks away. Future Nobita looks down on his son because he's basically like him when he was little. Then, something pops out on his mind about the sphere he found on Nobisuke's room, then starts questioning himself like \"Where\" and \"when\" did it happened. Suddenly, an unexpected visitor arrived, which is the present Nobita, and Doraemon. On the side, Nobisuke tried to type the number \"100\" (Nobita's Test Score), but it's still incorrect, just as he's about to give up, he jumps onto his bed, and found his father's manga \"Oshishi Mask\", which possibly he's about to type the correct code. Doraemon and present Nobita tries to help future Nobita about the sphere that his son found earlier, then finally, future Nobita remembers, which happened 25 years later in the present time. Flashback from the beginning of the episode, he came up and idea to store these treasure stuff he found on the shed, and Doraemon has a plan, he pulled out, \"Time Capsule\", this machine can store any things they want to put, like ice cream from a century that will never melt. Nobita decided to put his manga, ramen, and.... his pack of 0 test scores too. Outside, they buried the Time Capsule on the \"Pine Tree Of Yuradama\" from the present time. That's why they came to the future, to have future Nobita a chance to recall the memories with him. Suddenly, his mind begins to shudder when future Nobita remembered he put his pack of 0 test in the test scores in the Time Capsule, if Nobisuke finds about that, he will found out that future Nobita was lying this whole time, but Doraemon replied, \"He won't open the Time Capsule because of the password\", then Future Nobita stops. Present Nobita tells what password he put on the Time Capsule, and future Nobita now remembers, which is from the manga. Back on Nobisuke, he put the code \"044\" (O meaning 0, 4 means shi), then the Time Capsule opens. Inside, he took out a Cup of Ramen, and..... As soon they're about to go inside the room, future Nobita feels horrified of what he saw, and Nobisuke starts laughing really hard, and said \"What is this?\", but for Doraemon and present Nobita gone speechless and confuse, about why Nobisuke crack the correct code, all by himself. Then, future Nobita blames present Nobita for putting the test score on the Time Capsule, then present Nobita replied, \"How Should I Know?\", and Doraemon shrugs. Nobisuke continues to laugh, and the camera zooms to the manga, as the episode ends.\n\n== Characters ==\n* [[Nobita Nobi]]\n* [https://doraemon.fandom.com/wiki/Nobita_Nobi_(Future) Nobita Nobi (Future)]\n* [[Doraemon]]\n* [[Shizuka Minamoto]]\n* [[Shizuka Minamoto (Future)]]\n* [[Nobisuke Nobi (Nobita's son)]]\n* [[Yasashiku Gouda]]\n* [[Suneki Honekawa]]\n* [[Tamako Nobi]]\n* Random Person 1 (Old Man)\n* Random Person 2 (Park-Ranger?)\n\n== Gadgets Used ==\n* [[Time Capsule (Gadget)|Time Capsule]]\n* [[Take-copter]]\n* Mole Drill (Suneki's Gadget)\n== Trivia ==\n* This episode is a remake of the [[Doraemon (1979 anime)|1979 anime]] episode.\n* This is the third time Nobita visited his older self in the future. Meanwhile, Nobita is still at school redoing his homework, whining because he is fed up with it. He decides to sneak away home, but when he sees Sensei, he gets scared and runs away, causing Sensei to get angry. After running out of school, Nobita meets Shizuka and apologizes for being late, after which he runs away, causing her to wonder where he is in such a hurry.\n\nOn the way home, Nobita thinks about finishing his homework right away after returning home and apologizing to the teacher tomorrow, when suddenly a baseball falls on his head, and Gian and Suneo ask Nobita to throw it. Angry, Nobita throws it to them, but ends up at Kaminari's house and escapes. He goes outside and starts scolding the boys for breaking the window. Gian and Suneo make excuses that Nobita broke it, to which Kaminari demands to bring him here. Gian, Suneo, Yasuo and Haruo start chasing Nobita, but the latter manages to hide. Then Gian decides to put up posters about Nobita being wanted and the boys split up. Nobita realizes that Gian has declared him wanted and cries bitterly.\n\nAt this time, Sensei calls Tamako and reports to her about Nobita. She understands his words, and when she hangs up, she starts getting angry. Doraemon, who overheard the conversation, takes out the Safety Guidance Map and Enemy Stopper to know where Nobita is. He finds him and starts helping him get home safely. Nobita has had all sorts of experiences: either a dog chases him, or he almost runs into the boys looking for him. When he reaches the trash can, it turns out that Gian was waiting there to hit Nobita. But before Gian can hit Nobita, his mother suddenly stops him and forcibly takes him home, to which Nobita becomes calm. Doraemon suggests that he quickly run home, but when Nobita runs, he sees that there are two black chips there, meaning danger, and suddenly hears Tamako angrily shouting Nobita's name. Doraemon goes down and sees Sensei and Tamako scolding him for running away from school without permission. The episode ends with Doraemon concluding that Nobita was late in returning home, while Tamako and Sensei continue to scold him, and Nobita plaintively apologizes to them.\n\n== Characters ==\n=== Main ===\n*[[Doraemon]]\n*[[Nobita Nobi]]\n=== Supporting ===\n*[[Takeshi Gouda]]\n*[[Suneo Honekawa]]\n*[[Shizuka Minamoto]]\n*[[Sensei]]\n*[[Tamako Nobi]]\n=== Minor ===\n* [[Yasuo Tanaka]]\n* [[Haruo Musashi]]\n* [[Kaminari]]\n* [[Gian's mother]]\n\n==Gadgets used==\n* [[Safety Guidance Map]]\n* [[Enemy Stopper]]\n\n== Locations ==\n* [[Nobis' Residence]]\n* [[School]]\n* [[Open Lot]]\n* [[Kaminari's House]]\n* [[Mountain]]\n\n==Names in other languages==\n{| class=\"fandom-table\"\n|+\n!Language\n!Name\n!Meaning\n|-\n|Hindi\n|Nobita's Great Escape\n|Nobita's Great Escape\n|}\n\n== Video ==\n<gallery>\nFile:Doraemon 1979 - At the back of the console is a portal leading to the space station, which is enclosed by a thick, semi-spherical door with a hand wheel. The console is predominantly baby pink in color.\n\nThe rocket launching unit consists of a small rocket standing vertically, supported by a tower beside it, all mounted on a thick blue circular base.\n\nThe toolbox contains various items used to build the space station, including brown tape and scissors, as well as the two end sections of the space station. One of the end is a spherical door unit featuring a hand wheel which had four rectangular transparent glass windows and the rear section is a circular aft section with propellers like that of a rocket.\n\nAfter the space station is built, inside it there is another pink console and space suits installed in it. The console is comparatively smaller and is connected to the station by a wire. The space suit is an ordinary space suit consisting of a huge oxygen bag and glass helmet.\n\n[[Doraemon]] and [[Nobita Nobi|Nobita]] had a simple white cylindrical station with it's top end being the portal and rear end being the propeller.\n\n[[Takeshi Gouda|Gian]] and [[Suneo Honekawa|Suneo]]'s station resembled Gian's face with his teeth showing out. The eyes served as glass windows. The nose was the portal end and at the back of his head was the propeller end.\n\n<gallery align=\"center\">\nNCSS (366).png|[[Doraemon|Doraemon]] and [[Nobita Nobi|Nobita]]'s station\nNCSS (672).png|[[Takeshi Gouda|Gian]] and [[Suneo Honekawa|Suneo]]'station\n</gallery>\n\n==Features==\n===General===\n<gallery type=\"slideshow\">\nNCSS (234).png|Control and communication\nNCSS Station]]'', [[Doraemon]] and [[Nobita Nobi|Nobita]] created a space station together. Seeing a satellite being launched from [[Nobis' Residence]], [[Takeshi Gouda|Gian]] and [[Suneo Honekawa|Suneo]] bribe Doraemon with [[Dorayaki|dorayakis]] into giving them the gadget. Nobita invites [[Shizuka Minamoto|Shizuka]] in his space station and the them along with Doraemon enjoy their time in the station.  Gian and Suneo's station follow a dangerous orbit and they get trapped from the inside. The rest of the gang help them in getting out of there and both the stations eventually burn by the gravity.\n\n==Naming and Translation==\n\n==Appearances==\n*[[Nobita's Cardboard Space Station]] <small>(debuts)</small>\n\n==Trivia==\n\n==References==\n\n[[Category:Gadgets]]\n[[Category:Gadgets originated from 2005 series]]"}}}]},{"pageid":66050,"ns":0,"title":"Shadow Guard","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{GadgetInfobox \n|Gadget name = Shadow Guard \n|Japanese = かげながら \n|Rōmaji = Kagenagara\n|Image1 = Shadow guard.png\n|Owner = [[Doraemon]]\n|User = For [[Shizuka]]\n|Gadget Type = Invisible Robot\n|Features = A invisible robot created to protect people from any danger\n|First Appearance =[[The Invisible Bodyguard]]\n|Color=#e6e87a|Text color=#f3f7a6|Border color=#dde14c|Color light=#edee9b|Image2=Shadow guard1979.png}}\n'''The Shadow Guard''' is a gadget owned by Doraemon.\n\n==Function==\nWhen used, this shadow guard will protects that person from strangers, disasters and any dangers. The shadow guard will immediately take action to protect him/her if a disaster occurs. But he can only be visible in a dark place.\n\n==Usages==\n\n* Nobita is very worried about Shizuka who is alone at home. Doraemon and Nobita come to Shizuka's house to give this Shadow Guard to her. Then Nobita asking to Doraemon to do some experiments for that robot in order to make sure Shizuka his future wife is safe for any disaster suddenly occurs.\n\n==Appear in==\n*[[The Invisible Bodyguard]]\n[[Category:Gadgets]]\n[[Category:Invisible Gadgets]]"}}}]},{"pageid":69217,"ns":0,"title":"Leaf-Gazing Set","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{Stub}}\n\n'''Leaf-Gazing Set''' is a set of gadgets which the user can see through leaves.\n[[Category:Gadgets]]\n[[Category:Set of Gadgets]]"}}}]},{"pageid":69949,"ns":0,"title":"Chroma Key Set","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{GadgetInfobox\n| Gadget name = Chroma Key Set\n| Japanese = クロマキーセット \"Kuromakī setto\"\n| Image1 = Chroma Key set.png\n| Owner = [[Doraemon]]\n| User = [[Doraemon]], [[Nobita]]\n| Gadget Type = Video editor\n| First Appearance = [[Nobi-chan Man with Chroma Key]]\n}}\n'''The Chroma Key set''' is a set of gadgets owned by Doraemon which can create visual effect of the video. This gadgets contain a sattelite camera, 3 Dimensional video camera, monitor with the device , and A green screen with blue screen.\n\n== Function ==\n'''Chroma key compositing''', or chroma keying, is a visual-effects and post-production technique for compositing (layering) two images or video streams together based on color hues (chroma range). \n\nTo using this gadget, First of all, you need to launch the satellite camera, the satellite camera will record the scenery and can be seen on the monitor. Second set up the green screen as a background. Then, stand behind of the green screen while record the person with using a special camera and start the button to start Chroma key technique. This video now has special effects\n\nBut this gadget has a more special function than original one. When the user is recorded by a special camera while standing behind a green background, it can create a 3 dimensional of himself. Apart from that, there are still many interesting functions of visual effect you can try when using this one.\n\n== Debut ==\n\n* [[Nobi-chan Man with Chroma Key]]\n\n[[Category:Gadgets]]\n[[Category:Set of Gadgets]]"}}}]},{"pageid":70655,"ns":0,"title":"Hitler Pills","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{GadgetInfobox\n| Gadget name = Hitler Pills\n| Japanese = ヒトラーの錠剤\n| Image1 = \n| Rōmaji = Hitorā no jōzai\n| Owner = Doraemon\n| User = Doraemon\n| First Appearance = The Painting Valuing Machine\n| Image4 = Hitler Pills.png\n}}\n\n'''Hitler Pills''' is a gadget owned by [[Doraemon]]\n\n== Function ==\nWhen someone consumes one (or more) of the small red circular pills inside of the jar, They begin to act like Austrian-German dictator Adolf Hitler. In [[The Painting Valuing Machine]], Doraemon uses them to command a Nazi soldier to leave so he can save a young girl's weak grandfather.\n\n== Appears in ==\n\n* [[The Painting Valuing Machine]]\n\n== Trivia ==\n\n* Due to the references to Hitler and World War II in this gadget's debut episode, Many countries refused to dub it. Though a Spanish and Italian dub of the episode exists, despite Spain and Italy being historically fascists countries.\n* In the Spanish dub, They are called Dulces para aparentar ser Hitler (Sweets to pretend to be Hitler). They are referred to as sweets/candies rather than pills, Probably to avoid referencing drugs.\n* In the Spanish dub, Doraemon introduces himself as the Führer (German term for 'leader') to the soldier rather than introducing himself as simply \"Doraemon\" like he does in the original Japanese version.\n* This gadget never appeared outside of it's debut episode (Which was never remade for the 2005 Anime).\n[[Category:Gadgets]]\n[[Category:1979 Anime]]"}}}]},{"pageid":70738,"ns":0,"title":"The Karma Horse","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{1979 Anime}}{{EpisodePrevNext|Anime series=1979|Previous episode=Gourmet Tabletop|Next episode=Time Floodgate|Color=CC845A|Color light=FFA500|Border color=B37450}}{{EpisodeInfobox|episode=The Karma Horse|color=#CC845A|text color=|animeimage=0603_TheKarmaHorse-1981.png|tcimage=|japanese=サイ オー {{ruby|馬|うま}}|romaji=Sai ō uma|broadcast=[[TV Asahi]]|series=|airdate=August 24, 1981|season=3|number=603|overall=|manga=|opening=|ending=|insert=|writer=|story=|animedirector=|director=|previous=|next=}}\n\nThe Karma Horse (サイ オー {{ruby|馬|うま}}  / Sai ō uma ) is the an episode of the [[Doraemon (1979 anime)|Doraemon 1979 anime]].\n\n== Synopsis ==\n\n== Plot ==\n\n== Characters ==\n\n== Gadgets Used ==\n\n== References =="}}}]},{"pageid":73982,"ns":0,"title":"Secret Gadget Encyclopedia (Nobita and the Green Giant Legend DS)","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"\n{| class=\"fandom-table\" border=\"1\" cellspacing=\"1\" cellpadding=\"1\" style=\"width:100%;\"\n|-\n! scope=\"col\" |No.\n! scope=\"col\" |Gadget\n! scope=\"col\" |Picture\n! scope=\"col\" |Description\n|-\n|01\n|Ataru Gun (アタールガン)\n|[[File:NDS2008Gadget01.png|150px]]\n|\"Because it's balls have eyes, no matter how hard it tries to run away, it'll always get hit. Fascinated by its ability to create giant drawings and messages, Nobita soon begins using it to torment Gian and Suneo.\n\n== Plot ==\nThe episode begins at a bakery, where Nobita buys two meat buns and two curry buns before heading home. Along the way, he encounters Shizuka, who is on her way to a bookstore. Nobita happily accompanies her and spends some time browsing manga while she selects a book. After finishing her shopping, Shizuka tells Nobita it is time to leave. Nobita says he wants to stay a little longer, causing Shizuka to walk ahead by herself. However, he quickly changes his mind and follows her. As they walk together, Nobita asks if he can come over and play before she starts reading her new book. Suddenly, Shizuka notices strange words written on the concrete road. The message reads, \"Hurry up and go home!\" Neither of them knows where it came from. Moments later, another message appears saying, \"Shizuka is causing trouble.\" As more mysterious warnings continue appearing on the road, Nobita becomes increasingly frightened. The messages seem to know exactly where he is and what he is doing. Terrified, Nobita finally runs away screaming, leaving a confused Shizuka behind as the messages mysteriously erase themselves.\n\nWhile fleeing through the neighborhood, Nobita encounters additional messages that seem to follow his movements. Convinced that something supernatural is happening, he rushes home and begs Doraemon for help. Doraemon, however, is more concerned about the meat buns in Nobita's bag, which have become cold and stale after being carried around for so long. Back in Nobita's room, Doraemon reveals that he was responsible for the messages all along. He introduces a gadget called Nasuka-kun, a device inspired by the famous Nazca Lines of Peru. Using a satellite-like display, the gadget allows users to write giant messages and drawings directly onto the landscape. To demonstrate, Doraemon zooms in on Japan and then onto Nobita's neighborhood. He writes Nobita's name on the rooftop using the gadget, and the message instantly appears in reality. Amazed, Nobita eagerly takes over and begins experimenting with the device himself.\n\nHis first target is Gian. After zooming in on Gian's house, Nobita writes \"Gorilla\" across the rooftop and laighs. At the same time, Gian is secretly sneaking away from his own store. The messages appear around him, seemingly calling him by name and asking where he is going. Thinking some mysterious force is watching him, Gian becomes terrified. Nobita then draws arrows leading Gian's mother directly to her son, allowing her to catch him before he can escape. Encouraged by his success, Nobita turns his attention to Suneo. This time, he simply draws a long black line behind him. As Suneo walks through town, the line continuously follows him wherever he goes. At first he dismisses it as a coincidence, but the line keeps matching his movements perfectly. The realization that the line is chasing him eventually becomes too much, and Suneo runs away in panic while Nobita laughs from home. Later, Gian and Suneo compare their experiences and realize the strange events have one thing in common: Nobita. Meanwhile, Nobita becomes distracted by thoughts of Shizuka and uses Nasuka-kun to draw an '''Aiaigasa''', the traditional symbol of two people sharing an umbrella that is often associated with romance, by drawing on top of Shizuka's house's rooftop.\n\nSoon afterward, Nobita receives a phone call from Suneo inviting him to come play games. Believing everything is normal, Nobita heads out to meet his friends. However, Gian and Suneo suddenly appear and reveal that the invitation was a trap. Having discovered his involvement with the mysterious drawings, they get their revenge by doodling all over Nobita's face. Humiliated, Nobita returns home in tears. Instead of learning his lesson, he decides to retaliate once again.\n\nThe following morning, television stations across Japan report a bizarre phenomenon. Huge black drawings and messages have appeared across various parts of the country, covering roads, fields, buildings, and entire neighborhoods. While Tamako and Nobisuke watch the shocking news reports, Nobita casually leaves for school. On the way, he encounters Gian and Suneo discussing the strange event. Nobita calmly greets them and continues walking. Once Nobita's \"casual face\" is out of sight, he grins and quietly remarks that they have no idea who did it. Back at home, Doraemon watches the truth. Nobita used Nasuka-kun on a much larger scale, covering parts of Japan with giant drawings and insulting messages aimed at Gian and Suneo, including giant caricatures and labels such as \"Gian the Gorilla\" and \"Suneo the Kitsune.\" Then, a small image of Nobita laughing mischievously appears on the screen, revealing that he is entirely responsible for the nationwide phenomenon, as the episode ends.\n\n== Characters ==\n\n* [[Doraemon]]\n* [[Nobita Nobi]]\n* [[Shizuka Minamoto]]\n* [[Suneo Honekawa]]\n* [[Takeshi Gouda]]\n* [[Tamako Nobi]]\n* [[Nobisuke Nobi]]\n* [[Tsubaki Gouda|Gian's Mother]]\n* Unnamed Bakery Lady\n* Unnamed News Reporters\n\n== Gadgets Used ==\n\n* [[Nasuka-kun (The Nazca Lines) (gadget)]]\n\n== Trivia ==\n\n* The episode title '''\"Nasuka-kun\"''' is a pun based on the Nazca Lines, combining the archaeological landmark with a friendly gadget-style name.\n* The gadget's name and the episode title are references to the famous Nazca Lines, a series of giant geoglyphs in Peru.\n* An \"Aiaigasa\" drawing appears in the episode, a traditional Japanese symbol often used to represent romantic feelings between two people.\n\n== References ==\n{{EpisodePrevNext|Anime series=1979|Previous episode=The Mammoth Came Out!|Next episode=Mom Apron|Color=6e856a|Color light=7f997a|Border color=5f735c}}"}}}]}]}}
